# Validation: ASHRAE Standard 140

OpenSimula is tested with the method of test of ANSI/ASHRAE Standard 140-2023, *Standard Method of Test for the Evaluation of Building Energy Analysis Computer Programs*. For each output the Standard asks for, the OpenSimula result is compared with the range (minimum and maximum) of the example results of the reference programs published with the Standard.

The range shows how much well-established programs differ from each other; it is not the Standard's formal acceptance criteria. A result outside the range is a prompt to look into that case, not necessarily an error. A value counts as inside when it is within the range widened by 0.1 % of its bound, to allow for the rounding of the reference spreadsheets.

*Generated by `ASHRAE_140/validation/validate.py` with OpenSimula 0.8.9 on 2026-10-29. The test case definitions are in the `ASHRAE_140` folder of the repository.*

## Building thermal envelope and fabric load tests (Section 7)

Reference programs: BSIMAC, CSE, DeST, EnergyPlus, ESP-r, TRNSYS (example results from `Std140_TF_Results.xlsx`). **184 of 219 values inside the reference range; 1171 of 1397 profile points (hourly, monthly…).**
